- **oauth-refresh-token-single-use-debug-burns-cred**: OAuth refresh tokens (GitHub, Notion, Slack, …) are SINGLE-USE — calling `provider.refresh(refresh_token)` from a debug REPL or one-shot script without persisting the returned new tokens to DB silently invalidates the credential. The new tokens vanish at process exit, the OLD refresh in DB is now rejected by the provider, every subsequent dispatch fails with `bad_refresh_token` / 401. Only a fresh OAuth flow recovers. Treat refresh as a database-side transaction, not a function call. ([blas1n/claude-skills](https://github.com/blas1n/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/oauth-refresh-token-single-use-debug-burns-cred) · ★2)
